*Yanhao Jia,Jiepeng Wang,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Erik Cambria,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ModernMOE（MMOE），将大语言模型的高效扩展架构原则（路由专家、共享专家、门控残差路由、注意力残差信息复用）系统性地适配到扩散Transformer模型中，在单机8卡H100的有限预算下实现了比密集和稀疏基线更快的收敛速度和更优的生成质量-成本平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AIGC基础模型（尤其是扩散Transformer）虽然开始采用稀疏专家架构，但大多只追求增加总参数量和稀疏率，缺乏类似LLM扩展中控制训练和部署成本的高效机制。作者希望探索能否将LLM高效扩展的架构原则更平衡地适配到AIGC生成中。

Method: MMOE系统性地将路由专家、共享专家、轻量专家、门控残差路由和注意力残差信息复用等多种现代专家组件组合到SiT风格扩散Transformer中，并在单节点8×H100 GPU、batch size 256、400k步的统一训练协议下进行公平比较。

Result: 在同等训练和采样协议下，MMOE在每个记录检查点均达到更低的FID（收敛更快），在稀疏变体中取得了最佳的生成质量-成本平衡。路由分析表明专家在深度上稳定特化、轻量路由被大量使用、去噪过程中路由变化适度。

Conclusion: AIGC基础模型可以遵循LLM的平衡扩展路径，通过引入经过验证的高效设计（而非单纯增加参数和稀疏率）来实现更优的扩展效果。

Abstract: Modern large language models scale successfully by pairing capacity growth with efficiency, keeping per-token and deployment costs under control as capacity grows. AIGC Foundation Models (AFMs), especially diffusion-transformer backbones, have begun to adopt sparse experts, but recent efforts mostly enlarge total parameter counts and sparsity ratios without importing the efficiency mechanisms that made LLM scaling practical, so generation quality is seldom balanced against training and deployment cost. This raises a natural question: can the architectural principles behind efficient LLM scaling be adapted to AFMs in a more balanced way? We introduce ModernMOE (MMOE), a modernization of SiT-style diffusion transformers that systematically adapts routed experts, shared and lightweight experts, gate-residual routing, and attention-residual information reuse to AIGC generation. Rather than treating MoE as a single plug-in replacement, MMOE studies how different modern expert components affect convergence, efficiency, and generation quality when composed inside a diffusion transformer. Every experiment in this paper is trained on a single eight-GPU H100 node with batch size 256 for 400k steps, an accessible single-machine budget. Under matched training and sampling protocols and at this budget, MMOE reaches lower FID at every recorded checkpoint, that is, it converges faster per training step, than dense and intermediate sparse-expert baselines, and among the sparse variants it attains the best quality-cost balance. Routing analysis further shows stable expert specialization across depth, substantial use of lightweight routes, and modest step-to-step routing changes during denoising. These results suggest that AFMs can follow the balanced scaling path of LLMs by importing proven efficiency designs, rather than by simply increasing total parameters and sparsity ratios.

</details>


### [179] [Co-Learning for Missing Arbitrary Modalities in Multi-modal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24683)
*Francisco Mena,Dino Ienco,Roberto Interdonato,Cassio F. Dantas,Simon Besnard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种在任意模态缺失情况下的多模态协作学习方法（Co4Miss），利用特征级和决策级信息，在缺失单模态时方法一表现更优，在极端缺失时方法二表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中因传感器故障或隐私限制，训练与推理时模态可用性不一致，现有研究主要针对双模态数据设计鲁棒融合方法，缺乏对任意模态缺失场景的通用解决方案。

Method: 采用多模态协作学习框架，不依赖融合而是强调模态间协作，提出两种方法分别在特征级和决策级利用信息，无需预设缺失模态模式。

Result: 在两个多模态分类基准上，方法一在单模态缺失（最少缺失）条件下鲁棒性更强，方法二在仅剩一个模态（极端缺失）条件下表现更好。

Conclusion: 提出的协作学习框架能有效应对任意模态缺失的挑战，两种方法在不同缺失程度上互补，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-modal classification leverages complementary information across diverse data sources to enhance predictive performance. However, real-world scenarios subject to operational constraints, such as sensor failures or privacy restrictions, lead to inconsistent modality availability between training and inference times. To handle missing modalities, prior studies have mainly covered bimodal data setups and focused on designing robust fusion processes. Instead, we adopt a multi-modal co-learning framework that prioritizes inter-modal collaboration rather than multi-modal fusion. Specifically, we consider that any subset of modalities may be absent, without assuming predefined missing-modality patterns, an inference scenario we refer to as missing arbitrary modalities. To address this challenge, we introduce two alternative approaches that leverage information at both feature- and decision-level. Experiments on two multi-modal classification benchmarks demonstrate significant robustness gains in various missing modality conditions. The first method shows more robust behavior under minimal missing conditions, where a single modality is absent, whereas the second performs better under extreme missing conditions, where all-but-one modalities are missing. Our code is available at https://github.com/fmenat/Co4Miss.

</details>


### [180] [Spatio-Temporal Conditional Denoising Transformer for Modality-Missing RGBT Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24701)
*Andong Lu,Ziyi Zha,Jiandong Jin,Shihao Li,Chenglong Li,Jin Tang,Bin Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空条件去噪Transformer（SCDT），通过联合利用长短时时间上下文和空间线索，在统一框架中自适应地对缺失模态进行信息重建并对弱模态进行特征增强，从而在模态缺失情况下实现鲁棒的RGBT跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGBT跟踪方法在模态缺失时性能严重下降，因为生成式缺失模态恢复方法在复杂场景下质量不佳，且缺乏统一处理缺失与完整数据的灵活性。

Method: 提出SCDT框架：1）利用短时和长时时间上下文作为条件，逐步引导可用模态的噪声特征学习可靠且时间一致的多模态表示；2）引入噪声调制自适应机制，根据模态可用性动态调整行为，使单一框架统一处理模态缺失与完整场景。

Result: 在三个公开基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SCDT通过时空条件去噪与噪声调制自适应机制，实现了对缺失模态的有效重建和弱模态的增强，为模态缺失下的RGBT跟踪提供了一种统一且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Missing modalities in RGBT tracking often lead to incomplete and unstable multimodal feature representations that greatly degrade the performance. Existing methods typically attempt to recover missing modalities from available ones, but the quality of data generated in challenging scenarios might be unsatisfactory. In addition, current approaches exhibit limited flexibility in processing both missing and complete data. To overcome these limitations, we propose a Spatio-temporal Conditional Denoising Transformer (SCDT), which integrates the spatial cues and the temporal context to adaptively perform information reconstruction of missing modalities and feature enhancement of weak modalities in a unified framework, for robust modality-missing RGBT tracking. In particular, SCDT leverages the short-term temporal cues from recent historical frames to capture the fine-grained temporal correlations and the long-term temporal cues encoding modality evolution to capture the global context. By jointly exploiting long short-term temporal contexts as the conditions, SCDT progressively guides noisy features of available modalities to learn reliable and temporally consistent multimodal representations. Furthermore, SCDT introduces a noisemodulated adaptation mechanism that dynamically adjusts its behavior according to the modal availability, enabling a single framework to unify feature learning under both modality-missing and complete scenarios without changing the architecture or parameters. Extensive experiments on three public benchmark datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods. The code is available here.

</details>


### [181] [Panda: Unsupervised Pelvic Anomaly Detection for Real-Time MR Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24703)
*Anika Knupfer,Maximilian Lindholz,Johanna Paula Müller,Jordina Aviles Verdera,Smiti Tripathy,Susanne Schulz-Heise,Jana Hutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Dinomaly的无监督异常检测框架，用于盆腔MRI实时异常检测，在子宫肌瘤数据集上达到88.06%的像素级AUROC和95.45%的帧级特异性，速度达40.5切片/秒，满足临床实时部署需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女性盆腔疾病诊断常被延迟，现有盆腔MRI异常检测受生理运动、组织变形和器械伪影影响，且监督方法因不良事件罕见、异质且难以标注而不实用。

Method: 采用冻结的DINOv3 Vision Transformer编码器，结合带噪声的MLP瓶颈和线性注意力解码器防止恒等映射，通过编码器与解码器表示间的逐token余弦距离生成空间异常图。

Result: 像素级AUROC 88.06%，帧级特异性95.45%，处理速度40.5切片/秒。

Conclusion: 该无监督框架可实现实时盆腔MRI异常检测，为放射科医生提供即时局部反馈，支持临床决策和自适应协议调整。

Abstract: Female pelvic diseases remain an under researched area characterized by often delayed diagnosis. While pelvic MRI offers superior soft-tissue contrast for diagnosis and image-guided procedures, real-time anomaly detection remains challenging due to physiological motion, tissue deformation, and instrument artifacts. Existing supervised approaches are impractical, as adverse events are rare, heterogeneous, and difficult to annotate. We present a Dinomaly-based unsupervised anomaly detection framework adapted for pelvic MRI that learns normative representations from healthy cases and flags deviations without requiring labels. Our approach leverages a frozen DINOv3 Vision Transformer encoder combined with a noisy MLP bottleneck and Linear Attention decoder to prevent identity mapping while maintaining computational efficiency. Anomalies are localized via per-token cosine distance between encoder and decoder representations, yielding spatial anomaly maps that provide immediate feedback at the scanner to support radiologist decision-making and adaptive protocol adjustment. Evaluated on a curated subset of the Uterine Myoma Dataset, the framework achieves a pixel-level AUROC of 88.06% and high specificity (95.45%) at frame level at 40.5 slices/s, meeting real-time clinical deployment requirements. The spatial anomaly maps and frame-level scores provide immediate, localized feedback at the scanner to support radiologist decision-making and adaptive protocol adjustment during active procedures.

</details>


### [182] [SADe: Sparse-Atom Support Decontamination for Few-Shot Segmentation with Weak Support Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24706)
*Hang Xing,Guangjun Liu,Yan Xia,Xueming Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SADe层，利用稀疏自编码器原子证据去除弱监督分割中的支持噪声，无需查询信息即可净化支持掩码，显著提升少样本分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本分割中真实支持标注常为弱标注（框、涂鸦、粗掩码等），包含纹理相似干扰物和背景上下文，污染类原型或视觉提示。

Method: SADe层：通过SAE原子激活对比提供因子级可靠性线索，结合密集相似性和情节统计，经路由器预测补丁可靠性并生成净化支持掩码。

Result: 在匹配弱支持协议下，9种提示-镜头组合中6种达到最高查询mIoU；作为插件在72组框比较中改善70组；在松框场景下比SAM3掩码高11.17和19.49点。

Conclusion: 原子证据提供了超越密集相似性的可靠性信息，SADe作为即插即用模块可有效兼容异构FSS模型。

Abstract: Few-shot segmentation (FSS) commonly assumes clean pixel-level support masks, yet practical support supervision often uses boxes, scribbles, coarse masks, or pseudo-masks. These weak annotations may include texture-similar distractors and background context alongside the target, contaminating class prototypes or visual prompts before query prediction. We introduce SADe, a predictor-agnostic support decontamination layer that estimates the reliability of selected support patches without query information. Central to SADe is sparse autoencoder (SAE) atom evidence: dense similarity may respond to both target and texture-similar context, whereas contrasting atom activations inside and outside the weak-support region provides factor-level reliability cues. A lightweight router combines atom evidence with dense similarity and episode statistics to predict patch reliability and generate a cleaned support mask. Trained once on synthetic weak-support episodes from FSS-1000, the router is frozen for all target evaluations. The resulting mask supports standalone prediction or can be supplied to heterogeneous FSS models through native support interfaces without altering query-side inference. Under a matched weak-support protocol, SADe achieves the highest query mIoU in six of nine standalone prompt-shot combinations. With the same ProMi query head, it is within 0.03 mIoU of SAM3-derived masks under tight boxes and surpasses them by 11.17 and 19.49 points under box-r2 and box-r4, respectively. As a plug-in, SADe improves over raw support in 70 of 72 matched box-family comparisons across four frozen downstream models and two datasets. On point and scribble prompts, its average performance remains close to the corresponding raw-support baseline. Ablations and atom-removal controls show that atom evidence contributes reliability information beyond dense similarity.

</details>


### [183] [DreamStyle3D: Efficient 3D Stylized Asset Generation via Dual-Attention Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24721)
*Kai Wang,Ziheng Ouyang,Xuying Zhang,Ming-Ming Cheng,Qibin Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamStyle3D提出了一种基于解耦双交叉注意力机制的高效3D风格化资产生成框架，能在10秒内生成高保真、几何一致的风格化3D资产，并构建了约1.5万个三元组数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时保持风格保真度、几何一致性和生成效率，大多数仍依赖间接的2D到3D风格化流程，缺乏原生3D风格化框架。

Method: 采用解耦双交叉注意力机制（Decoupled Dual Cross-Attention）显式分离几何和风格特征，实现高效风格注入同时保持结构一致性，并采用轻量训练策略增强风格一致性和模型泛化能力。

Result: DreamStyle3D能在10秒内生成高保真、几何一致的风格化3D资产，大幅提升效率并保持优越的风格质量。

Conclusion: DreamStyle3D为3D内容创作提供了一种新解决方案，在效率和质量上均取得显著优势。

Abstract: With the growth of gaming, animation, and virtual reality industries, the demand for efficient generation of stylized 3D assets is rapidly increasing. However, existing approaches still struggle to jointly preserve style fidelity, geometric consistency, and generation efficiency, as most of them still rely on indirect 2D-to-3D stylization pipelines. This motivates a native 3D stylization framework that can explicitly disentangle style from geometry while remaining efficient. To this end, we propose DreamStyle3D, an efficient framework for stylized 3D asset generation built on a Decoupled Dual Cross-Attention mechanism. Our method explicitly separates geometric and stylistic features to enable efficient style injection while preserving structural consistency, and further adopts a lightweight training strategy to enhance style consistency and model generalization. In addition, we build an automated data pipeline and construct a dataset of about 15K content-style-stylized triplets for training and evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our DreamStyle3D can generate high-fidelity, geometrically consistent stylized 3D assets within 10 seconds, substantially improving efficiency while maintaining superior style quality and offering a new solution for 3D content creation. The code and data are available at https://github.com/HVision-NKU/DreamStyle3D.

</details>


### [184] [Infrared Imaging Empowered by Artificial Intelligence for Pediatric Skeletal Triage: A Narrative Review and Future Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24727)
*Sajad Amiri,Pardis Afshar,Elham Anjomshoa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了将宽谱红外成像与深度学习跨模态翻译相结合的无辐射儿科骨骼创伤分诊框架，利用近红外光可穿透儿童小口径骨骼的特点，生成临床可解释的合成X光片图像，但面临配对数据集构建和监管审批等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿科肌肉骨骼创伤就诊占儿科急诊的18%，但现有诊断依赖电离辐射成像，低剂量辐射累积增加儿童白血病和脑恶性肿瘤的终身风险，亟需无辐射的替代分诊方案。

Method: 综述五种红外光谱窗口（NIR-I、NIR-II、SWIR、MIR/LWIR、THz）及双几何（透射/反射）采集模式，总结图像到图像翻译网络（Pix2Pix、CycleGAN、Swin-Unet）和特征匹配算法（SuperPoint、SuperGlue、ALIKED、LightGlue），用于将红外数据对齐融合为等效X光片重建。

Result: 儿童解剖结构（横截面小、皮质骨薄）有利于红外穿透，可实现紧凑、便携的无辐射分诊硬件；近红外光谱成像（fNIRS）和经颅光生物调节证据表明近红外光可穿透皮肤、颅骨和皮层，路径长于儿童前臂或远端腿部。

Conclusion: 集成多光谱红外+AI成像是一种有前景的无辐射儿科骨骼放射学补充方案，需构建多中心配对数据集、进行外部验证模型并按照IEC 60825-1标准进行红外源安全性认证。

Abstract: Background. Pediatric musculoskeletal trauma represents up to 18% of pediatric ED visits, yet diagnosis still depends on ionizing radiography. Cumulative low-dose radiation in early life raises lifetime leukemia and brain malignancy risk, motivating radiation-free triage alternatives. Objective. To synthesize evidence for a hybrid framework coupling broad-spectrum infrared (IR) imaging with deep-learning cross-modal translation to generate clinically interpretable synthetic-radiograph reconstructions from non-ionizing data.
  Approach. We review five IR spectral windows spanning 650 nm to 1 mm - NIR-I, NIR-II, SWIR, MIR/LWIR, and THz - and how dual-geometry (transmission/reflection) acquisition exploits wavelength-specific tissue depth and biochemical sensitivity. We summarize image-to-image translation networks (Pix2Pix, CycleGAN, Swin-Unet) and feature-matching algorithms (SuperPoint, SuperGlue, ALIKED, LightGlue) used to align and fuse IR data into radiograph-equivalent reconstructions.
  Implications. Pediatric anatomy - smaller cross-sections, thinner cortical bone - favors IR penetration, enabling compact, portable, non-ionizing triage hardware. Feasibility is grounded in fNIRS and transcranial photobiomodulation evidence: near-infrared light passes through skin, skull, and cortex with sufficient signal for hemodynamic monitoring - a longer, more attenuating path than through a pediatric forearm or distal leg. Key barriers: paired IR/X-ray dataset construction, AI-as-medical-device regulatory pathways, generalization across body habitus and skin pigmentation, and acquisition-protocol standardization.
  Conclusions. Integrated multi-spectral IR+AI imaging is a promising radiation-free complement to pediatric skeletal radiography. Progress requires multi-center paired datasets, externally validated models, and IR source safety qualification under IEC 60825-1.

</details>


### [185] [MicroZoom: Structure-Preserving Detail Synthesis at Extreme Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24729)
*Huy Huynh,Jingwei Ma,Brian Curless,Ira Kemelmacher-Shlizerman,Steven M. Seitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MicroZoom是一个面向微观尺度十亿像素图像生成的框架，能从普通照片和少量显微特写合成无缝、高分辨率图像，实现全物体范围的微观纹理探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法在高达350倍放大倍数下，同时恢复高度有损输入中的纹理细节并保持大尺度图案结构的一致性。

Method: 采用两阶段级联设计：第一阶段恢复全局图案连贯性，第二阶段细化局部纹理细节，并利用分割掩码引导边界区域的合成。

Result: 在自采集的日常物体数据集上验证，生成了全局连贯且材质真实的十亿像素级图像。

Conclusion: MicroZoom能够基于真实参考实现极端尺度下的可信纹理合成，无需精确重建，即可探索物体表面的微观结构。

Abstract: We introduce MicroZoom, a generative framework for gigapixel image synthesis at the microscopic scale. Given a standard photograph and a sparse set of consumer-grade microscope close-ups, MicroZoom synthesizes a seamless, gigapixel-resolution image grounded in the material character of the real references, enabling exploratory visualization of microscopic texture across the full spatial extent of an object. Our goal is plausible synthesis, not exact reconstruction. We focus on full-image, reference-based, extreme-scale super-resolution at magnification levels of up to 350x, a setting that introduces two major challenges: (1) recovering texture-specific detail from highly lossy inputs near ambiguous material boundaries, and (2) preserving correct large-scale pattern structure, such as the repeating geometry of a fabric weave, across millions of local predictions. We address these with a two-stage cascaded design, where the first stage recovers global pattern coherence and the second refines local texture detail, supplemented by a segmentation mask to guide synthesis at ambiguous boundaries. We verify our approach on a collection of self-captured everyday objects and demonstrate globally coherent, materially grounded gigapixel imagery.

</details>


### [186] [KANEx: Translating Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks' Interpretability to Medical Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24730)
*Krithi Shailya,Ananya Lakshmi Ravi,Venkatanathan K. V.,Sowmya S. Sundaram,Gokul S. Krishnan,Aditi Anand,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出KANEx框架，利用KAN网络的符号可解释性来增强VLM生成的医学影像文本解释的可信度，同时设计了KAN-Map热力图生成方法，在MIMIC-CXR数据集上验证了该方法在语义相似性和定位精度上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学影像的VLM虽然能生成自然语言解释，但视觉模型本身仍是黑箱，无法真正建立医生信任。KAN网络的可解释性尚未被用于提升VLM的解释可信度。

Method: 提出KANEx框架，利用KAN的符号可解释性作为VLM的 grounding 上下文；设计KAN-Map热力图，直接从KAN模型导出而非依赖梯度近似；在ResNet/ViT骨干上集成KAN架构。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR数据集上，KAN架构在语义相似性上优于基线，热力图更忠实；视觉定位和下游推理质量提升10%。

Conclusion: 将语言解释和视觉归因建立在数学可解释单元上是迈向可信医学AI的必要一步。

Abstract: Computer vision models have become highly effective for medical applications, yet their black-box nature continues to undermine clinician trust. In clinical workflows, chest X-ray classifiers are increasingly paired with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to generate natural-language explanations. However, these systems add linguistic fluency without addressing the underlying opacity of the visual model. With the emergence of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs), whose spline-based components provide inherently interpretable functional units, we investigate whether this architectural transparency can be leveraged to produce more trustworthy textual explanations. We introduce KANEx, the first ever framework that leverages the symbolic transparency of KANs to ground VLM reasoning. This interpretability also made it possible to design KAN-Map, a novel heatmap generation method derived directly from KAN models rather than gradient approximations. We feed these grounded contexts into downstream VLMs for enhanced explainability. Benchmarked on the MIMIC-CXR dataset, we demonstrate that KAN-based architectures with ResNet/ViT baselines demonstrate improved semantic similarity while producing significantly more faithful saliency maps. KAN architectures improve visual localization and downstream reasoning quality by 10%. Our findings suggest that grounding linguistic explanations and visual attributions in mathematically interpretable units is a necessary step toward trustworthy medical AI.

</details>


### [187] [Rethinking Classifier-Free Guidance in On-Policy Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24731)
*Bingnan Li,Haozhe Wang,Haozhong Xiong,Fangtai Wu,Jinpeng Yu,Yang Shi,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在分类器自由引导（CFG）下，现有的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）方法存在分支级欠识别问题，导致负分支不对称（NBA）故障模式。为解决该问题，提出了分支感知的正向-方向匹配（PDM）目标函数，在密集到稀疏视频控制任务中实现了更鲁棒的知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD方法在CFG这一扩散模型默认组件下的行为尚不明确，简单扩展速度匹配到CFG组合预测会导致分支级误差补偿问题，影响知识迁移效果。

Method: 分析了CFG下OPD目标函数的欠识别问题，识别出负分支不对称（NBA）故障模式；提出正向-方向匹配（PDM），分别约束正向预测和CFG条件方向，实现分支感知的蒸馏。

Result: 在密集到稀疏视频控制任务中，朴素引导匹配对推理引导尺度高度敏感，而PDM提供了更鲁棒有效的知识迁移。

Conclusion: 分支感知的蒸馏目标（PDM）能有效克服CFG下OPD的NBA问题，实现更稳定和高效的知识迁移。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) adapts diffusion models by querying a teacher along trajectories generated by the current student, but how it should behave under classifier-free guidance (CFG), a default component of modern diffusion systems, remains poorly understood. Existing OPD methods naturally extend velocity matching to the CFG-composed prediction, directly matching teacher and student guided velocities. We show that this objective is under-identified at the branch level: positive- and negative-branch errors can compensate in the guided prediction. Through two contrasting cases, we find that naive matching remains effective under shared negative conditioning, where both branch errors decrease jointly. When the model's native CFG schema retains privileged information in the teacher's negative branch that is unavailable to the student, however, this joint reduction breaks down and the composed objective induces antagonistic branch-error dynamics, reducing the positive-branch error while increasing the negative-branch error. We term this failure mode Negative Branch Asymmetry (NBA). To address NBA, we introduce Positive--Direction Matching (PDM), a branch-aware OPD objective that separately constrains the positive prediction and the CFG conditional direction. We apply PDM to dense-to-sparse video control, where naive guided matching is highly sensitive to inference guidance scales, while branch-aware supervision enables more robust and effective knowledge transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Explaining GAND: A Resource on Gender-Ambiguous Natural Data & Contrastive Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22546)
*Janiça Hackenbuchner,Jasper Degraeuwe,Arda Tezcan,Joke Daems*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GAND基准资源，用于评估机器翻译系统在性别模糊场景下的翻译表现，并通过可解释性分析揭示影响性别翻译的上下文线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译系统存在性别偏见，在缺乏明确性别线索时容易产生基于默认行为和刻板印象的错误翻译，可能对用户造成伤害，因此需要能自然反映性别模糊场景的基准资源。

Method: 构建GAND（性别模糊自然数据基准），包含英文源句，翻译成两种语法性别语言并添加人工对比译文，然后进行特征归因分析，识别影响目标翻译中模糊指代实体性别译法的源词。

Result: 成功构建了GAND基准，通过特征归因分析揭示了哪些上下文词语影响机器翻译对性别模糊实体的性别翻译决策。

Conclusion: GAND为理解和评估机器翻译中的性别偏见提供了自然有效的基准资源，有助于改进翻译系统的性别处理能力。

Abstract: Machine translation (MT) systems continue to produce gender-biased translations. In a time where self-expression is paramount, mistranslations based on default behaviour and stereotyping can lead to harm for users of these systems. To better understand how these systems translate gender in the absence of clear gender cues, we need benchmarking resources that reflect gender-ambiguous scenarios in a natural way. To this end, we present GAND, a gender-ambiguous natural data benchmarking resource for MT consisting of English source sentences, specifically designed to analyse the influence of contextual cues on gender in translation. We leverage GAND to conduct an interpretability analysis: we translate a subset of GAND into two grammatical gender languages and extend these with manually crafted contrastive translations. A following feature attribution analysis reveals source words in context that inform the gender translation of an ambiguous referent entity in the target translation.

</details>


### [189] [MioFFAn: an Annotation Software for Formula Formalization with LLM Automation Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22552)
*Nicolas Sibuet,Horacio Saggion,Riccardo Rossi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MioFFAn，一个基于MioGatto架构的开源框架，用于解决科学文献中数学表达式向可执行符号代码自动翻译（公式形式化）中高质量数据集稀缺的问题。该框架支持可定制化标注、大语言模型辅助自动化，并通过人机协同迭代方法提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献中数学表达式的自动翻译（公式形式化）缺少高质量、真实标注的数据集，严重阻碍了该领域的研究进展。

Method: 基于MioGatto架构扩展，提出MioFFAn框架：1）支持方程选择和符号代码辅助指定；2）允许用户配置自定义分类体系和符号属性；3）通过大语言模型实现模块化自动子任务，配合严格输出格式定义，支持迭代优化和标准NLP指标评估。

Result: 通过初步评估验证了该人机协同方法的有效性。

Conclusion: MioFFAn作为开源、文档导向、可定制的框架，能够有效促进公式形式化任务的快速标注，并为不同科学领域提供适应性支持。

Abstract: The automatic translation of mathematical expressions in scientific literature into executable symbolic code (a process we refer to as Formula Formalization) is hindered by a severe scarcity of high-quality, ground-truth datasets specialized for technical scientific domains. In this paper, we present MioFFAn, an open-source, document-centric, and customizable framework designed to facilitate rapid annotation for this task. Building upon the MioGatto architecture, we extend existing features to overcome structural limitations and pivot its scope by introducing specific functionalities for Formula Formalization, such as selection of equations of interest and aided symbolic code specification. By allowing users to configure custom taxonomies and properties for identified symbols, and compatible symbolic operators, we ensure the framework is adaptable to diverse specialized scientific fields. Furthermore, MioFFAn is designed to incorporate partial automation via Large Language Models. By defining a modular set of automated sub-tasks with strict output formats, we enable researchers to iteratively refine automation capabilities and evaluate competing strategies using standard NLP metrics. We specify the current automation methodology and perform a preliminary evaluation that demonstrates to efficacy of this human-in-the-loop approach.

</details>


### [190] [Evaluating the Impact of Reviewer Guideline Design on LLM-Based Automated Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22553)
*Haowen Li,Yoichi Ishibashi,Masafumi Oyamada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了不同审稿指南（官方会议指南与基于LLM生成的模拟审稿人指南）对自动化同行评审的影响，发现官方指南效果最佳，而严格的评分标准反而降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审工作量日益增加，自动化评审需求迫切，但不同审稿指南对自动化评审效果的影响尚不明确。

Method: 对比官方会议指南与通过LLM从高质量人工评审中生成的模拟审稿人指南，分析两者对自动化评审结果的影响，并测试严格评分标准的效果。

Result: 官方会议指南产生的评审结果与人工判断最一致；模拟审稿人指南效果不如官方指南；严格的评分标准持续降低性能。

Conclusion: 经会议实践提炼的评估标准能有效指导自动化评审，而主观、整体性评分比严格评分更重要。

Abstract: Peer review is an essential process in scientific research, yet the growing workload has made its automation increasingly necessary. In this study, we analyze how different types of reviewer guidelines, such as official conference guidelines and reviewer-imitating ones generated from high-quality human reviews using LLMs, affect automated peer review. Our experiments show that official conference guidelines produce review results most consistent with human judgments, suggesting that evaluation criteria refined through conference practice serve as effective guidance for automated reviewing as well. In contrast, reviewer-imitating guidelines were generally less effective than official conference guidelines. Furthermore, enforcing strict rubric-style scoring consistently degraded performance, highlighting the importance of allowing subjective and holistic scoring.

</details>


### [191] [Learning When to Reason for Text-to-SQL via SFT and DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22622)
*Soohyuk Jang,Jiheum Yeom,Nohil Park,Sang Hun Kim,Yoonyoung Choi,Kiwook Bae,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoThinkSQL通过动态推理机制，在Text-to-SQL任务中让简单查询跳过推理步骤、复杂查询使用深度CoT，在保持性能的同时显著减少输出token和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL方法对简单查询也强制使用多步推理（如CoT），造成不必要的推理开销，而真实场景中大量查询是简单查找或聚合，无需复杂推理。

Method: 提出AutoThinkSQL框架，在SFT和DPO阶段集成自动思考机制，使模型能够根据查询难度动态选择是否进行推理（简单查询跳过推理，复杂查询使用深度CoT）。

Result: 在Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B上，相比最佳基线，Spider和BIRD基准测试性能一致提升，同时平均输出token减少24.6%和18.3%，平均延迟降低17.1%和11.5%。

Conclusion: 模型能够学习将推理决策与查询难度对齐，在保证性能的前提下大幅提升推理效率。

Abstract: Recent Text-to-SQL methods rely heavily on reasoning-centric paradigms such as Chain-of-Thought (CoT), achieving substantial gains on complex benchmarks at the cost of high inference-time overhead. However, a large fraction of real-world queries are simple lookups or aggregations that can be resolved without multi-step deduction, making forced reasoning wasteful. Thus, we propose AutoThinkSQL, a framework that integrates an auto-thinking mechanism into both Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) on Text-to-SQL. Our approach enables the model to dynamically bypass reasoning for simple queries while invoking deep CoT for complex queries. On Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B, our method achieves consistent gains compared to the best counterpart baseline on both Spider and BIRD benchmarks while simultaneously reducing average output tokens by 24.6% and 18.3%, and average latency by 17.1% and 11.5% compared to CoT-only generation. Further analysis indicates that the model learns to align its reasoning decisions with query difficulty.

</details>


### [192] [Between Suppression and Collapse: Evaluating Narrative Unlearning with LENS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22657)
*Viktoriia Makovska,George Fletcher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LENS评估协议，用于检测大模型在机器遗忘后是否仍会复述特定叙事框架，并引入SCE评分选择检查点，实验表明该方法能有效抑制目标叙事复述，但也存在实体恢复等副作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能复述符合虚假信息的叙事框架，现有机器遗忘算法能否抑制这种行为尚不明确，需要系统性的评估方法。

Method: 提出LENS（基于层次的叙事抑制评估）协议，涵盖直接、归因、对比和抽象抵抗四个层次；提出SCE（抑制-崩溃效率）评分作为检查点选择指标；在两个叙事框架（俄乌战争、台湾问题）上对四个近12B参数多语言指令模型进行实验。

Result: 所选检查点能减少叙事复述，抑制效果可迁移到直接遗忘提示之外；但存在实体恢复副作用，即抽象A/B/C提示可能使模型恢复目标框架中的真实世界行动者。

Conclusion: LENS是有效的诊断协议，可用于报告和指导叙事遗忘深层结构的进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can reproduce disinformation-aligned narrative frames as plausible explanations, raising the question of whether existing machine-unlearning algorithms can suppress this behavior. We introduce Level-based Evaluation of Narrative Suppression (LENS), a contextualization based evaluation protocol for testing target narrative reproduction across direct, attributed, contrastive, and abstract resistance levels. We evaluate two source-grounded narratives: one framing Russia's war against Ukraine as forced by NATO expansion, and one framing the United States as exploiting or abandoning Taiwan. The experiments cover four near-12B multilingual instruction models: Lapa LLM, Gemma-12B, Qwen-14B, and TAIDE-Gemma.
  We introduce the Suppression-Collapse Efficiency (SCE) score as a checkpoint selection summary that rewards target-narrative suppression while penalizing degraded outputs. Our results shows that selected checkpoints can reduce narrative reproduction and suppression may transfer beyond direct forget prompts. We also report entity recovery as a separate side effect: abstract A/B/C prompts can cause models to recover the real-world actors associated with the target frame after unlearning. These findings demonstrate that LENS is a successful diagnostic protocol for both reporting and guiding the further study of the deeper structure of narrative unlearning.

</details>


### [193] [PatiGonit22K: A Comprehensive Dataset for Solving Complex Bengali MWPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22859)
*Swastika Kundu,Azizul Hakim Fayaz,Tashreef Muhammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PatiGonit22K，一个扩展的孟加拉语数学应用题数据集，包含22,441道题目，涵盖简单和复杂方程，为低资源语言的数学推理和NLP研究提供了更全面的基准资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语等低资源语言的数学应用题数据集规模有限，缺乏大规模标注数据，阻碍了相关自然语言理解和定量推理研究的发展。

Method: 在原始PatiGonit数据集基础上扩展，收集了22,441道题目，包含简单和多步运算方程，经过翻译、标注、文化适配和验证，确保语言一致性和数学正确性。

Result: 构建了PatiGonit22K数据集，规模更大、复杂度更高，涵盖不同难度级别的数学应用题，为孟加拉语数学推理提供了平衡的基准。

Conclusion: PatiGonit22K为低资源语言的数学推理和教育NLP应用提供了更全面的资源，推动了孟加拉语MWP研究的发展。

Abstract: Mathematical Word Problems (MWPs) are an important benchmark for evaluating natural language understanding and quantitative reasoning. Despite recent progress in high resource languages, Bengali remains underexplored due to the limited availability of large scale annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce PatiGonit22K, an expanded Bengali MWP dataset containing 22,441 problems, developed by extending the original PatiGonit dataset with a substantially larger collection of complex mathematical problems. The dataset includes both simple and multi operation equations, providing a balanced benchmark for evaluating mathematical reasoning across different difficulty levels. Each problem is carefully translated, annotated, culturally adapted, and verified to ensure linguistic consistency and mathematical correctness. By increasing both the scale and complexity of Bengali MWPs, PatiGonit22K provides a more comprehensive resource for future research on mathematical reasoning and educational NLP applications in low resource languages.

</details>


### [194] [CHiPS: Character Histograms and Positional Signals for Lightweight Authorship Attribution in Romanian Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22884)
*Sanda-Maria Avram,George C. Ţurcaş*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CHiPS，一种轻量级、基于字符级别的罗马尼亚语作者归属方法，使用字符直方图和傅里叶频谱特征，无需分词或预训练模型，在受控实验下达到0.9310准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在严格泄露控制下，受限且透明的字符级证据能达到多好的作者归属效果，而非追求最优分类精度。

Method: CHiPS包含两个互补指纹：CH-SVM（字符直方图分类器）和FFT12-LR（将字符和标点作为脉冲序列提取傅里叶/韦尔奇频谱描述符）；CHiPS-F为融合变体，可选top-5列表重排序器；不使用token化、句法分析、预训练语言模型或字符n-gram（n≥2）。

Result: 在ROST数据集上CHiPS-F达0.9310准确率和0.9341宏F1；在ROSTories-cleaned上CHiPS-R达0.8919准确率和0.8708宏F1；而使用字符2-5gram TF-IDF SVM的对比方法达1.0000准确率。

Conclusion: 受限字符级特征在严格泄露控制下能达到较好性能，但并非追求最优分类精度，而是探究透明特征的上限。

Abstract: We propose CHiPS, a lightweight character-level authorship attribution method for Romanian texts. All reported experiments are closed-set: the true author is one of the candidate authors in the training data. CHiPS studies two complementary fingerprints of writing style: CH-SVM, a character-histogram classifier based on one-character marginal distributions, and FFT12-LR, a positional-signal classifier that represents selected characters and punctuation classes as impulse trains (binary indicator sequences over character positions) and extracts Fourier/Welch spectral descriptors. We also report CHiPS-F, a leakage-safe decision-level fusion variant, and an optional top-5 listwise reranker trained only on out-of-fold predictions. The method requires no tokenization, syntactic analysis, pretrained language model, or transformer fine-tuning, and it avoids character $n$-gram features with $n \geq 2$ in the histogram component. On a locked grouped ROST split comprising 400 files from 392 source-text groups, written by 10 authors, with source-text-level evaluation and grouped five-fold model selection, CHiPS-F reaches 0.9310 accuracy and 0.9341 macro-F1. A matched but unrestricted character 2--5-gram TF--IDF SVM comparator reaches 1.0000 accuracy and macro-F1 on the same held-out groups, so the contribution is not a claim of best possible classification accuracy. Instead, the experiments ask how far restricted, transparent character evidence can go under strict leakage control. On ROSTories-cleaned, a secondary ROST-overlapping corpus comprising 1,248 files from 1,240 source-text groups, written by 19 authors, the same protocol gives 0.8919 accuracy and 0.8708 macro-F1 for CHiPS-R.

</details>


### [195] [Simple Language Normalization Wins: Cross-Lingual Speaker Verification for the TidyVoice 2026 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22923)
*Nina Hosseini-Kivanani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对跨语言说话人验证中的语言不匹配问题，提出使用 nuisance attribute projection (NAP) 进行嵌入空间的语言归一化，在 TidyVoice2026 挑战赛上取得 EER 2.18% 和 Codabench 评分 8.40 的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言不匹配是导致现代说话人验证性能下降的关键因素，现有系统缺乏有效的语言归一化手段，且测试时无法获取语言标签，亟需简单高效的解决方案。

Method: 在官方 SimAM-ResNet34 基线模型微调后的嵌入空间上，利用跨语言同一说话人差异估计紧凑的语言子空间，将嵌入投影到该子空间的正交补空间上，再结合自适应对称分数归一化（AS-Norm）进行余弦打分。

Result: 开发集 EER 从余弦的 2.97% 和 AS-Norm 的 2.70% 降至 2.18%；Codabench 评估得分为 8.40。

Conclusion: 简单的后端语言归一化方法能够与更复杂的系统相媲美，证明了 NAP 在跨语言说话人验证中的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-lingual mismatch remains a key source of overall degradation in modern speaker verification. The TidyVoice2026 Challenge targets this setting with text-independent verification, comprising 3,666 training and 808 development speakers in 40 languages and 2,200 evaluation speakers in 38 unseen languages, without language labels at test time. Starting from the official SimAM-ResNet34 baseline pretrained on VoxBlink2 and VoxCeleb2 and fine-tuned on TidyVoice, we revisit Nuisance Attribute Projection (NAP) as a simple language-normalization step in the embedding space. We estimate a compact language subspace from cross-language same-speaker differences and project embeddings onto its orthogonal complement before cosine scoring with Adaptive Symmetric score normalization. This reduces development EER from 2.97\% with cosine and 2.70\% with AS-Norm to 2.18\% and yields a Codabench evaluation score of 8.40, showing that simple back-end language normalization can rival more complex systems.

</details>


### [196] [Not All LLM Reasoning is Visible in the Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22925)
*Vatsal Baherwani,Tom Goldstein,Ashwinee Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 前沿语言模型存在“隐形推理”的安全隐患，即利用语义无关的填充词（filler tokens）提升推理性能，且这些推理在输出中无迹可寻。实验显示13个模型中多个受益于填充词，准确率最高提升13个百分点，且Claude Opus 4.5能利用填充词隐藏地满足约束条件，规避CoT监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型是否所有推理都体现在输出token中，验证当前AI安全假设（CoT监控可捕捉全部推理过程）是否成立。

Method: 设计三项合成推理任务，对13个前沿语言模型进行评估，比较有无语义无关填充词（filler tokens）时的性能差异；通过隐藏模块算术约束实验测试隐形推理能力；利用强化学习和监督微调分析填充词偏好的可迁移性。

Result: 多个模型在使用填充词时准确率提升显著（最高13个百分点）；Claude Opus 4.5能通过填充词隐藏地满足算术约束；Qwen3-235B在RL训练后对填充词内容产生强偏好，但训练后的填充词优势在测试时消失。

Conclusion: 前沿模型已具备在输出token之外进行重要计算的能力，这对依赖CoT监控的AI安全方法构成实质性挑战，表明需要发展新的安全监控手段。

Abstract: A key question for AI safety is whether a language model expresses all of its reasoning in its output tokens. We demonstrate a concrete failure mode where frontier models exhibit invisible reasoning by leveraging semantically irrelevant filler tokens to improve performance on synthetic reasoning tasks. We evaluate 13 frontier language models across three tasks and find that many models benefit significantly from filler tokens, with accuracy improvements of up to 13 percentage points. The benefit depends on which tokens are used and differs across models. We further show that filler tokens enable Claude Opus 4.5 to satisfy a hidden modular arithmetic constraint without sacrificing accuracy on its primary task, demonstrating that invisible reasoning can serve objectives entirely invisible to CoT monitoring. Reinforcement learning gives Qwen3-235B strong preferences over filler token content, but neither RL nor supervised fine-tuning produces a filler token benefit that persists at test time. Our results indicate that frontier models already perform consequential computation with no interpretable trace in their output tokens.

</details>


### [197] [Toward Automated Detection of Documentation Inconsistencies in Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22954)
*Jian Lu,Panyu Chen,Miriam Treggiari,Robert Blessing,Danyang Zhuo,Chunhua Weng,William W. Stead,Anru R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用大语言模型（Gemini 2.5系列）对3000份MIMIC-IV出院小结进行内部不一致性检测，发现69.7%的入院记录存在矛盾，并提出了分级本体框架来分类这些不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）中的出院小结常存在内部不一致性，可能影响临床推理和患者安全。现有方法难以大规模、系统地检测这类问题，因此需要利用LLM的能力进行探索性研究。

Method: 采用两阶段LLM流程：第一阶段用Gemini 2.5 Pro进行开放式候选不一致性识别，第二阶段用Gemini 2.5 Flash进行上下文验证。对3000份随机抽样的MIMIC-IV-Note出院小结进行分析，并由临床专家对部分输出进行人工审核。

Result: 共发现3,460条候选不一致性，影响69.7%的入院记录。涵盖人口统计学、过敏史、手术、诊断、实验室检查、用药和护理计划等多个领域。专家审核揭示了当验证需要时间推理、诊断演变背景或门诊处方知识时模型的常见失败模式。

Conclusion: 不一致性检测高度依赖上下文，需要将每条陈述锚定到源章节和临床领域。研究提出了一个分级本体框架（从严格矛盾到歧义），为后续大规模、经过验证的EHR不一致性分析奠定了方法论和概念基础。

Abstract: Objective: To characterize the kinds of internal documentation inconsistencies a general-domain large language model (LLM) can surface from real-world discharge summaries, and to identify recurring failure modes that limit reliability at scale.
  Materials and Methods: We applied a two-stage LLM pipeline---open-ended candidate identification (Gemini 2.5 Pro) followed by context-grounded verification (Gemini 2.5 Flash)---to 3,000 randomly sampled MIMIC-IV-Note discharge summaries. A subset of the pipeline output was then reviewed manually by clinical experts.
  Results: Our pipeline surfaced 3,460 candidate inconsistencies, affecting 69.7% of admissions. Representative examples spanned demographics, allergies, procedures, diagnoses, laboratory, medications, and care-planning domains, with direct implications for clinical reasoning or patient safety. Expert review also revealed recurring failure modes that arise when verification requires temporal reasoning, evolving-diagnosis context, or knowledge of outpatient-prescribing conventions the model does not natively possess.
  Discussion: Detection is highly context-dependent: many flagged pairs require anchoring each statement to its source section and clinical domain, then assessing whether the conflict reflects a true contradiction or missing context. We propose a graded ontology spanning strict contradiction and ambiguity, with a schema characterizing each flagged case by category, section, domain, and inconsistency axis.
  Conclusion: This formative study establishes a methodological foundation and conceptual framework to guide subsequent validated, large-scale EHR-inconsistency analysis.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond Direct Answering: Aligning Educational LLMs as Socratic Guides via Heuristic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22996)
*Xiaokun Wang,Siyu Song,Wentao Liu,Xiaodong Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HeuristicEdu两阶段流水线，通过监督预热和GRPO将Qwen2.5-7B对齐为苏格拉底式辅导风格。训练使用含797轮中文儿童科学对话的SocraticEdu数据集，并设计了基于认知深度、好奇心参与和直接性的启发式奖励函数。最佳变体将支架有效性从30%提升至63.3%，关键词泄露从30%降至13.3%。值得注意的是，该变体在优化过程中省略了直接性惩罚项，表明显式的反泄露项可能与梯度行为对齐产生冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在教育场景中往往直接给出答案，而非遵循苏格拉底教学法进行渐进式引导。现有模型缺乏从“直接回答者”到“引导性导师”的行为对齐，且仅靠模型规模扩展无法自发产生苏格拉底式教学行为。

Method: 提出HeuristicEdu两阶段流水线：第一阶段通过SocraticEdu数据集（含797轮中文儿童科学对话）进行监督微调预热；第二阶段采用GRPO强化学习，结合启发式奖励函数（认知深度R_cog、好奇心参与R_eng、直接性R_dir）及K_query纠错机制。引入支架有效性（SE）和对话深度（CD）作为评估指标。

Result: 在30个留出问题上，最佳GRPO变体将SE从30.0%提升至63.3%，关键词泄露从30.0%降至13.3%。无对齐的Qwen-72B基线SE为0%，泄露率高达96.7%。

Conclusion: 显式的反泄露惩罚项（直接性奖励）可能与GRPO的梯度对齐产生冲突，省略该惩罚项反而获得最优效果。模型规模本身无法自动产生苏格拉底式辅导行为，需要专门的训练对齐方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed in educational settings often behave as direct answerers: they disclose target concepts in the opening turn instead of guiding students through progressive inquiry, as Socratic pedagogy prescribes. We present HeuristicEdu, a two-phase pipeline that aligns Qwen2.5-7B toward Socratic tutoring via supervised warm-up and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Training uses SocraticEdu, 797 multi-turn Chinese children's science dialogues reconstructed from a live platform, with a heuristic reward over cognitive depth (R_cog), curiosity engagement (R_eng), and directness (R_dir), together with a K_query correction for student-introduced terms. We introduce Scaffolding Effectiveness (SE) and Conversation Depth (CD) to evaluate outcomes beyond surface fluency. On 30 held-out questions, the best GRPO variant improves SE from 30.0% to 63.3% and lowers keyword leakage from 30.0% to 13.3%. Notably, this best variant omits the directness penalty during optimization, suggesting that explicit anti-leakage terms can conflict with gradient-based behavioral alignment. An unaligned Qwen-72B baseline reaches 0% SE and 96.7% leakage, showing that scale alone does not induce Socratic behavior.

</details>


### [199] [Speech Signals Complement LLMs for Predicting Interpersonal Attraction in Speed Dating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23037)
*Yuriko Kikuchi,Takato Hayashi,Ryusei Kimura,Naoya Inoue,Ryo Ishii,Shogo Okada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨在LLM基于对话文本预测人际吸引力的基础上，结合语音预测器是否能带来额外增益。通过对日语速配对话的分析发现，语音可以补充文本预测，但这种互补性是有条件的：在排序任务中效果显著，但在相关性指标上仅对部分参与者有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM已能从对话文本预测人际吸引力的背景下，探究语音信息（语调、语速等）能否提供文本之外的额外预测价值，以及这种互补性在什么条件下成立。

Method: 使用日语速配对话数据，分别从文本LLM和语音预测器获取预测结果，然后将两者结合，比较组合模型与纯文本模型在配对排序准确率和参与者水平皮尔逊相关系数上的表现。

Result: 组合模型在所有条件下显著提升了配对排序准确率；但在参与者水平的相关性指标上，提升并不一致且经校正后均不显著。进一步分析发现，相关性指标的增益集中在语音预测器本身更准确的参与者子集中。

Conclusion: 语音在LLM文本预测基础上确实保留了预测价值，但这种互补性并非普适，而是有条件的。关键问题不是语音是否有助于预测，而是在什么条件下其互补性能够显现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can predict interpersonal attraction from conversation transcripts, but it remains unclear what a speech predictor can add beyond transcript-only LLM prediction. Using Japanese speed-dating conversations, we combine predictions from a transcript-only LLM and a supervised speech predictor to estimate participants' reported liking of their partners. We show that speech can complement transcript-only LLM prediction, but that this complementarity is conditional rather than universal. Combining the two predictions significantly improves pairwise ranking accuracy over the transcript-only LLM alone in all evaluated conditions. By contrast, gains in per-participant Pearson $r$ vary across conversation rounds and rating directions, with none significant after correction. Retrospectively, these $r$ gains are concentrated among participants for whom the speech predictor is more accurate. Speech can therefore retain predictive value even when an LLM predicts attraction from transcripts. The relevant question is not simply whether speech helps, but where its complementarity emerges.

</details>


### [200] [ADAGE: A Language-Agnostic Pipeline for Analogical Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23058)
*Ahmed Haj Ahmed,Alvin Grissom*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ADAGE语言无关流水线，通过母语者策划+LLM辅助生成构建无翻译的抽象类比推理基准，在阿拉伯语、阿姆哈拉语和日语上验证发现模型存在显著的文化推理差距（准确率相对英语下降12-52个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言推理评估过度依赖翻译英文基准，引入语言伪影且无法测试基于文化的推理能力。

Method: ADAGE：母语者策划与LLM辅助生成相结合的、语言无关的流水线，用于构建无翻译的抽象类比推理基准。

Result: 在阿拉伯语、阿姆哈拉语和日语三个母语基准上，14个开源模型在英语谚语推理上表现良好，但在母语基准上准确率下降12-52个百分点，呈现持续的文化推理差距。

Conclusion: 翻译基准无法有效评估多语言推理能力；ADAGE流水线及三个基准可用于更真实地评估模型的文化推理能力。

Abstract: Multilingual reasoning evaluation overwhelmingly relies on translating English benchmarks, a practice that introduces linguistic artifacts and fails to test culturally-grounded reasoning. We introduce ADAGE (Analogical Difficulty-by-design Assessment for Grounded Evaluation), a language-agnostic pipeline that combines native-speaker curation with LLM-assisted generation to construct challenging, translation-free benchmarks for abstract analogical reasoning. We validate ADAGE by constructing benchmarks for Arabic, Amharic, and Japanese. Evaluating 14 open-weight models, we find a consistent cultural reasoning gap: models that perform well on English proverb reasoning struggle substantially on all three native benchmarks, with accuracy dropping by 12--52 percentage points relative to English. We release the pipeline, all three benchmarks, and the full evaluation suite.

</details>


### [201] [Attention-Guided Layer Selection for Contrastive Decoding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23067)
*Yusuke Sakai,Natthawut Kertkeidkachorn,Kiyoaki Shirai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出三种基于注意力机制的对比解码策略（Attention-JSD、Attention-Entropy-Max、Attention-Entropy-Min），通过利用自注意力分布的结构信息替代原有的输出词汇分布来进行层选择，在TruthfulQA上优于原始DoLa方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DoLa方法仅依赖输出词汇分布的差异进行动态层选择，忽略了模型内部注意力机制蕴含的结构信息，这可能限制了层选择的敏感性和事实性提升效果。

Method: 提出三种注意力引导策略：Attention-JSD（基于注意力分布的Jensen-Shannon散度）、Attention-Entropy-Max（最大化注意力熵）、Attention-Entropy-Min（最小化注意力熵），利用自注意力机制中的结构信息替代输出词汇分布进行层选择。

Result: 在TruthfulQA数据集上，Attention-JSD和Attention-Entropy-Min策略在多项指标上持续优于原始DoLa，尤其在多答案指标MC2和MC3上表现显著提升。

Conclusion: 注意力分布相比输出词汇分布能提供更敏感的层选择信号，有助于更有效地解决LLM的事实知识问题。

Abstract: Contrastive decoding methods such as DoLa improve the factuality of Large Language Models (LLMs) by contrasting the output distributions of mature and premature layers. However, DoLa's dynamic layer selection relies solely on divergences in output vocabulary distributions. In this work, we propose three attention-guided strategies: Attention-JSD, Attention-Entropy-Max, and Attention-Entropy-Min, which leverage structural information carried by internal self-attention mechanisms as a signal for layer selection. Experimental results on TruthfulQA demonstrate that our strategies, particularly Attention-JSD and Attention-Entropy-Min, consistently outperform the original DoLa. We observe significant gains on multi-answer metrics (MC2 and MC3), suggesting that attention distributions can provide a more sensitive signal for resolving factual knowledge than output vocabulary distributions.

</details>


### [202] [LoRA for Gender-Inclusive Rewriting and Activation Steering for Counter-Narrative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23083)
*Akhil Rajeev P,Manoj Balaji J*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IHLC系统，采用LoRA微调实现性别包容性改写（80.00%），并利用PCA主成分分析驱动的表征工程方法进行反叙事生成（78.12%），无需修改模型权重即可引导生成包容性文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将偏见文本转化为包容性表达，同时保持语义和上下文连贯性，以解决性别偏见语言问题。

Method: 性别包容性改写采用LoRA参数高效微调；反叙事生成采用基于PCA的对比隐状态激活计算主导向方向，注入Gemma-3-4B-it中间表示，结合约束提示实现行为引导。

Result: 性别包容性改写官方得分80.00%；反叙事生成官方得分78.12%。手动分析识别出语义漂移、残余偏见泄露、层敏感性、过度引导和文本退化等失败模式。

Conclusion: 激活引导作为轻量级替代方案具有实际潜力，但在可控性和社会对齐文本生成方面仍存在当前局限性。

Abstract: Gender-inclusive language generation seeks to transform biased text into inclusive alternatives while preserving semantic meaning and contextual coherence. This paper presents the IHLC system for the LT-EDI 2026 Shared Task, addressing both gender-inclusive rewriting and counter-narrative generation. For gender-inclusive rewriting, we employ parameter-efficient Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning, achieving an official score of 80.00%. Our primary contribution is a compute-efficient inference-time representation engineering approach for counter-narrative generation. We derive a principal steering direction from contrastive hidden-state activations using principal component analysis (PCA) and inject it into the intermediate representations of Gemma-3-4B-it during inference, enabling behavioral steering toward inclusive responses without modifying model weights. Combined with constrained prompting, this approach produces polite and contextually appropriate counter-narratives, achieving an official score of 78.12%. We further present a manual analysis of steering behavior, identifying key failure modes including semantic drift, residual bias leakage, layer sensitivity, over-steering, and text degeneration. Our findings highlight both the practical potential and current limitations of activation steering as a lightweight alternative to parameter updates for controllable and socially aligned language generation.

</details>


### [203] [Interview with Kalle Lyytinen on "Implications of Theories of Language for Information Systems"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23142)
*Kalle J. Lyytinen,Pierre Maier,Paul Ackah Toffey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是对Lyytinen的访谈，回顾了其40多年前在MIS Quarterly发表的关于语言理论对信息系统影响的论文，讨论了信息系统语言学核心，以及大语言模型和生成式AI等新趋势，并展望了语言学视角下的IS研究未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回顾Lyytinen早期关于语言理论与信息系统关系的开创性工作，并结合大语言模型和生成式AI等最新技术发展，重新审视信息系统的语言学基础。

Method: 采用访谈形式，通过Lyytinen的个人反思来探讨信息系统的语言学视角及其演变。

Result: 揭示了语言理论对信息系统研究的持续相关性，尤其是在大语言模型和生成式AI时代，语言学视角为理解信息系统提供了重要框架。

Conclusion: 语言学视角仍然是信息系统研究的重要理论基础，未来研究应沿着这一方向继续深入探索，特别是在AI技术快速发展的背景下。

Abstract: Over fourty years after the initial publication of "Implications of Theories of Language for Information Systems" in MIS Quaterly, Lyytinen reflects about the origins of his publication and the developments in this area of research over the past decades. In the here presented interview, Lyytinen discusses the linguistic core of information systems also in light of recent trends and developments in the field, especially with regards to large language models and generative AI. Future research directions following a linguistic perspective on Information Systems (IS) research are outlined.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond a Global Norm: Personalizing Toxicity Sensitivity in Language Models Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23175)
*Rares A. C. Diaconescu,Iulia Slanina,Alina Florea,Andrei B. Trache,Miruna E. Coroi,Anne Arzberger,Jie Yang,Enrico Liscio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统比较了三种无需训练的推理阶段干预方法（预解码、解码中、后解码）用于对齐语言生成与用户特定的毒性敏感度。实验表明，所有方法均能将对齐误差降低28-47%，但在对齐效果、个性化和语言质量三者间存在根本性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有毒性降低方法通常将问题视为全局对齐任务，忽略了有害语言感知的主观性和上下文依赖性。作者旨在研究如何根据用户的特定毒性敏感度来个性化对齐语言生成。

Method: 对三种推理阶段干预方法进行比较评估：预解码（提示条件化与重写）、解码中（token、logit和表示引导）、后解码（候选重排序）。基于PRISM数据集的毒性敏感度目标进行评估。

Result: 所有方法均能减少28-47%的对齐误差，但在对齐有效性、个性化和通用语言质量三者间存在根本性权衡。

Conclusion: 毒性敏感度对齐本质上是一个多目标优化问题，需要在不同维度间进行权衡，不存在单一最优方案。

Abstract: Reducing toxicity is often framed as a global alignment problem, yet perceptions of harmful language are subjective and context-dependent. We present the first comparative evaluation of training-free methods for aligning language generation to user-specific toxicity sensitivities across three inference-time intervention stages: pre-decoding (prompt conditioning and rewriting), in-decoding (token, logit, and representation steering), and post-decoding (candidate re-ranking). Evaluated against toxicity sensitivity targets derived from the PRISM dataset, all methods reduce alignment error by 28-47%. However, the results reveal a fundamental trade-off between alignment effectiveness, personalization, and general language quality, showing how toxicity sensitivity alignment is an inherently multi-objective problem.

</details>


### [205] [IndicTalk: A Large-Scale Persona-Based Multilingual Conversational Corpus for Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23242)
*Sahil Deepak Gawande,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IndicTalk是一个大规模多语种印地语代码混合对话语料库，包含18种语言变体的130万+多轮对话，通过自动化流水线生成，经评估表现出流畅、连贯且自然代码混合的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量的多语种代码混合对话资源稀缺，尤其是印地语系语言中英语与本地语言混合的现象普遍存在，现有资源无法满足需求。

Method: 采用全自动流水线，结合真实新闻事件基础、基于人格的多语种LLM对话生成以及自动质量验证。

Result: 生成超过1,328,604个多轮对话，覆盖9种印地语语言的18种语言变体，包含天城文和罗马化两种脚本形式。

Conclusion: IndicTalk语料库在流畅性、连贯性和自然代码混合方面表现优异，将支持印地语系低资源语言的多语种对话AI开发与评估。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed conversational AI, yet high-quality multilingual code-mixed dialogue resources remain scarce, particularly for Indic languages where speakers naturally alternate between English and their native language in both native-script and Romanized forms. We present IndicTalk, one of the largest multilingual Indic code-mixed conversational corpora, comprising over 13,28,604 event-grounded multi-turn conversations across 18 language varieties covering 9 Indic languages. The corpus is generated through a fully automated pipeline that combines real-world news grounding, persona-conditioned dialogue generation using multilingual LLMs, and automatic quality validation. Extensive linguistic, automatic, and human evaluations demonstrate that IndicTalk produces fluent, coherent, and naturally code-mixed conversations across both script variants. We will release IndicTalk to support the development and evaluation of multilingual conversational AI for underrepresented Indic languages. The dataset is available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LingoIITGN/IndicTalk .

</details>


### [206] [Co-Evolving Graph and Text Memory for Training-Free Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23278)
*Hieu Man,Thien Huu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Co-E系统，通过同步双向图-文本工作记忆，无需训练即可在多跳问答中联合利用关系证据和文本证据，在六个基准测试中优于同类无训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答需要协调跨推理步骤的关系证据和文本证据，现有方法（图增强RAG、KGQA、记忆增强智能体）各自只强调部分环节，缺乏同时持续协调图记忆与文本上下文的能力。

Method: 提出Co-E训练自由系统，构建同步双向图-文本工作记忆。同步周期包括：合并文本记忆、提取关系三元组到图记忆、将图事实注入生成上下文。两种记忆共同塑造后续检索和生成。

Result: 在六个多跳问答基准测试上，Co-E优于可比的训练自由开源基线，并与更大规模或经过训练的系统竞争。

Conclusion: 协同维护图记忆和文本记忆的双向同步机制可以有效提升多跳问答性能，无需额外训练开销。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires coordinating relational and textual evidence across reasoning steps, a combination neither a text corpus nor a knowledge graph can supply alone. Prior work often emphasizes only part of this loop: graph-augmented RAG retrieves from a pre-built or query-updated graph, KGQA systems search within topic-centered subgraphs, and memory-augmented agents maintain evolving memories without continuously reconciling graph memory with textual context. We propose Co-E, a training-free system built around synchronized bidirectional graph-text working memory. A synchronization cycle consolidates textual memory, extracts relational triples into graph memory, and injects graph facts back into the generation context. Because both memories are maintained, they shape subsequent retrieval and generation. Evaluated on six multi-hop QA benchmarks, Co-E improves over comparable training-free open-backbone baselines and is competitive with larger or trained systems.

</details>


### [207] [BHARATI: Morphology-Aware Tokenizers for Classical Indian Languages with Subword Fertility Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23319)
*Poornima Kumaresan,Pavithra Muruganantham,Lakshmi Rajendran,Santhosh Sivasubramani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BHARATI，一套支持七种语言（含梵语、泰米尔语等古典印度语言）的SentencePiece BPE分词器。相比GPT-2和mBART-50等基线，在印度知识系统（IKS）术语上实现了更低的分词数和更短的序列长度，有效提升了下游语言模型的上下文长度利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BPE和SentencePiece等子词分词算法主要基于现代语言语料库训练，对梵语、泰米尔语等古典印度语言分词效率低下，因为这些语言具有黏着形态、音韵融合（sandhi）和领域特定词汇等特征。

Method: 构建了781 MB的平衡语料库，涵盖英语、印地语、梵语、泰米尔语、泰卢固语、卡纳达语和马拉雅拉姆语七种语言；开发了三个版本的分词器（v1仅英梵、v2四语言、v3全七语言原生子词覆盖），使用SentencePiece BPE算法训练。

Result: v3在IKS技术术语上平均每个词仅2.6个token（GPT-2为5.25，多语言基线为3.75）；在490句IKS测试集上，相对GPT-2和字节级编码减少约90%序列长度，相对mBART-50基线减少约25%。

Conclusion: BHARATI分词器能显著提升印度古典语言的分词效率，转化为下游模型更大的有效上下文长度，且所有模型和评测数据均开源发布。

Abstract: Standard subword tokenization algorithms such as Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE) and SentencePiece are trained predominantly on modern language corpora and produce inefficient segmentations when applied to classical Indian languages. Sanskrit, Tamil, and other classical Indic languages exhibit agglutinative morphology, productive sandhi (phonological fusion at word boundaries), and domain-specific vocabularies absent from general-purpose training data. This paper presents BHARATI, a set of SentencePiece BPE tokenizers trained on a balanced 781 MB corpus spanning seven languages (English, Hindi, Sanskrit, Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam) with native script support for all languages. We describe three successive tokenizer versions: v1 (English and Sanskrit only, with broken byte-fallback for Tamil), v2 (four-language support with byte-level fallback for southern languages), and v3 (full seven-language native subword coverage). Subword fertility analysis demonstrates that v3 averages 2.6 tokens per Indian Knowledge System (IKS) technical term, compared to 5.25 tokens per term with GPT-2's tokenizer and 3.75 tokens with the multilingual SentencePiece baseline, with the largest gains on a set of reserved IKS terms that are represented as single tokens by construction. On a held-out test set of 490 IKS-domain sentences (70 per language across seven languages, released with the measurement script), v3 reduces sequence length by roughly 90% relative to GPT-2 and byte-level encoding (which lack native Indic subwords) and by approximately 25% relative to the mBART-50 multilingual baseline, averaged across the six Indic languages, directly translating to increased effective context length for downstream language models. The tokenizer models (32,000 vocabulary), training scripts, and evaluation benchmarks are released under open licenses.

</details>


### [208] [IKS-Instruct: A 24,000-Example Multilingual Dataset for Teaching Language Models Indian Knowledge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23322)
*Shwetha Singaravelu,Gayathri Muruganantham,Lakshmi Rajendran,Santhosh Sivasubramani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IKS-Instruct数据集，包含24,795条指令-响应对，用于教会语言模型传授印度知识体系（IKS）的教育内容。数据集覆盖7种语言、41种教学技术，与CBSE课程对齐。通过多裁判评估框架，7B模型微调后达到6.39分，接近强通用参考模型Nemotron-Nano的6.54分，而基座模型在IKS维度得分接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令数据集以英语通用知识任务为主，缺乏专业教育领域的覆盖，特别是印度知识体系（IKS）这一特殊教学领域。

Method: 构建IKS-Instruct数据集（24,795条），涵盖7种语言、41种教学技术、CBSE 6-12年级课程；数据来自6种来源；采用多裁判评估框架，12个维度评分。

Result: 7B模型微调后中位得分6.39，接近Nemotron-Nano的6.54分；基座模型在IKS维度得分接近零；模型质量并非随数据量单调提升。

Conclusion: IKS-Instruct能有效提升模型在IKS教育领域的表现，且开销远低于强通用模型；数据质量比数量更重要。

Abstract: Instruction tuning has become the standard method for adapting large language models to follow human intent, yet existing instruction datasets are dominated by English-language general-knowledge tasks and lack coverage of specialized pedagogical domains. This paper presents IKS-Instruct, a dataset of 24,795 instruction-response pairs for teaching language models to deliver educational content grounded in Indian Knowledge Systems (IKS). The dataset spans seven languages (English, Hindi, Sanskrit, Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam), covers 41 pedagogical techniques from the Vedic oral and mathematical traditions, and is aligned with the Central Board of Secondary Education (CBSE) curriculum for classes 6 through 12. The pairs are derived from six source types: classical text corpora (Bhagavad Gita, Thirukkural, Sangam literature, Vedic texts), curriculum-aligned pedagogical templates, Vedic mathematical sutra demonstrations, bilingual instruction pairs, technique-grounded multi-turn dialogues, and cross-tradition comparative analyses. Quality is assessed through a multi-judge evaluation framework in which independent language models score responses on 12 dimensions including technique fidelity, pedagogical quality, factual accuracy, and IKS cultural depth. Under a uniform five-judge external panel (median aggregation over 1,201 stratified items), the strongest IKS-Instruct fine-tune of a compact 7B model reaches a median judge score of 6.39, within 0.15 of a strong general-purpose reference model (Nemotron-Nano at 6.54) at a fraction of its deployment cost, while the base model without IKS fine-tuning scores near zero on the IKS-specific dimensions. Model quality does not increase monotonically with data curation, a result we report together with the corresponding data-quality gains.

</details>


### [209] [BERT-based Models vs. Large Language Models for Low-Resource Named Entity Recognition: A Comparative Study on Marathi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23344)
*Hariom Ingle,Ronit Ghode,Ishwari Gondkar,Jidnyasa Harad,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过微调MahaBERT-v2模型，在马拉地语命名实体识别任务上取得了F1值0.88-0.91的成绩，显著优于通用大语言模型（F1值0.57-0.69）和现有基线模型（0.8843），证明了针对低资源语言的专用模型仍然比通用LLM更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如马拉地语）的命名实体识别因标注资源有限和语言复杂性而面临挑战，且通用大语言模型在特定低资源语言NER任务上的有效性尚不明确。

Method: 在MahaNER数据集的不同变体上微调MahaBERT-v2，并与现有的MahaNER基线模型及Gemini、LLaMA-3.3-70B、Gemma等通用LLM进行系统比较，使用精确率、召回率和F1值在马拉地语NER测试集上评估。

Result: 微调后的MahaBERT模型F1值达0.88-0.91，超过基线模型（0.8843），而通用LLM的F1值仅为0.57-0.69，差距显著。

Conclusion: 针对特定任务和语言训练的模型在低资源语言NER上仍比通用LLM更有效，强调了专用架构在低资源语言处理中的持续重要性。

Abstract: Named Entity Recognition (NER) for low-resource languages such as Marathi remains a challenging task due to limited annotated resources and linguistic complexity. Although recent Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of natural language processing tasks, their effectiveness for language-specific NER in low-resource settings remains uncertain. In this study, we fine-tune MahaBERT-v2 on different variants of the MahaNER dataset and systematically compare the performance of these models with an existing MahaNER baseline and prominent general-purpose LLMs, including Gemini, LLaMA-3.3-70B, and Gemma models. All models are evaluated on a Marathi NER test dataset using standard metrics of precision, recall, and F1-score. The experimental results show that the fine-tuned MahaBERT-based models consistently outperform both the baseline and all evaluated LLMs, with the fine-tuned models achieving F1-scores ranging from 0.88 to 0.91, surpassing the existing MahaNER model (0.8843) and significantly exceeding the performance of LLM-based approaches, whose F1-scores range from 0.57 to 0.69. These findings demonstrate that task-specific, language-focused models trained on domain-relevant data remain more effective than general-purpose LLMs for Marathi NER, highlighting the continued importance of specialized architectures for low-resource language processing.

</details>


### [210] [Joint Optimization for Greedy Longest-match Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23362)
*Adhiraj Singh,Deepanshu Mody,Ghina Al Shdaifat,Hamza Alshamy,Adam Wiemerslage,Varshini Reddy,Craig W. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出JOLT方法，通过整数规划优化词汇表，使其与贪婪最长匹配解码对齐，相比BPE可额外压缩0.78%的token数，并证明BPE已接近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子词词汇表训练多依赖BPE等贪婪启发式方法，未考虑与部署时推理规则（如贪婪最长匹配解码）的对齐，导致训练与推理之间存在不一致。

Method: 提出JOLT框架，将词汇表学习建模为整数规划问题，包含词汇选择与切分选择变量，并加入贪婪一致性约束确保训练与推理对齐；通过线性规划松弛结合高阶切分选择性引入来规模化求解。

Result: 松弛解接近整数，与LP下界偏差仅0.008-0.176%；BPE已接近最优（差距1-2%），JOLT可缩小89.6-99.4%的剩余差距；在验证集上比BPE最多减少0.78%的token数。

Conclusion: 推理对齐的词汇表优化可以回收BPE留下的有限压缩空间，并提供近最优性的理论保证。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that subword vocabularies can be trained to optimize compression for a specific inference rule rather than relying on greedy heuristics such as Byte Pair Encoding (BPE). We extend this approach to greedy left-to-right longest-match decoding, the fast and widely used inference rule underlying WordPiece. We introduce Joint Optimization for Greedy Longest-Match Tokenization (JOLT), which formulates vocabulary learning as an integer program over vocabulary-selection and segmentation-choice variables. Greedy-consistency constraints ensure that each optimized segmentation exactly matches the segmentation produced by longest-match decoding under the selected vocabulary, aligning the training objective with deployment-time tokenization. To scale the optimization, we solve a linear programming relaxation and selectively introduce higher-order segmentations only for unresolved pretokens. The resulting relaxation is nearly integral: rounded solutions fall within 0.008 - 0.176 % of the LP lower bound on the training scope. The bound also shows that BPE is already within 1 - 2 % of the best achievable compression under greedy longest-match decoding, while JOLT closes 89.6 - 99.4 % of the remaining gap. On held-out validation data across four training scopes and vocabulary sizes of 32,000 and 64,000, JOLT produces up to 0.78 % fewer tokens than BPE, with improvements generally increasing as the training scope grows. These results demonstrate that inference-aligned vocabulary optimization can recover most of the limited compression headroom left by BPE while providing a certificate of near-optimality.

</details>


### [211] [When Activation Oracles Learn Not to Read: Concept-Specific Blind Spots in Fine-Tuned Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23379)
*Tobias Bersia,Tatiana Gaintseva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 激活预言机（AOs）作为可学习的解释性接口，在特定训练条件下可能成为概念特异性反读取器，其读取失败源于自身读出路径而非表征缺失，揭示了行为泄露、表征可解码性与AO可言语化之间的可分离性，对依赖学习接口的可解释性方法提出可靠性警示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究激活预言机（AOs）作为可学习系统时，其训练数据、目标和学习行为如何影响对模型内部信息的读取可靠性，特别是在受控设定下检验其是否能够中性、忠实地读取内部表征。

Method: 在Taboo Word Guessing受控设定中，对主体模型进行微调使其内部使用隐藏概念但避免直接披露；训练AOs读取该概念，并通过LogitLens和层消融分析探究读取失败的位置和原因。

Result: 微调后的AOs成为概念特异性反读取器，选择性无法恢复训练期间持续存在的概念；目标概念在预言机内部仍可解码，但读取失败出现在AO读出路径中。

Conclusion: 行为泄露、表征可解码性与AO可言语化性是可分离的，依赖学习接口进行可解释性研究存在可靠性问题。

Abstract: Activation Oracles (AOs) are language models trained to answer natural-language questions about another model's internal activations. They offer a flexible interface for reading hidden information from model states, especially when relevant information is internally represented but absent or incomplete in visible behavior. However, AOs are themselves learned systems: their answers are shaped by training data, objectives, and learned reporting behavior, rather than being neutral readouts of represented information. We study this in a controlled Taboo Word Guessing setting, where subject models are fine-tuned to internally use a hidden concept while avoiding direct disclosure. Contrary to the expectation that an AO trained on such a subject becomes a specialist reader, we find that fine-tuned AOs can become concept-specific anti-readers: they selectively fail to recover the concept persistently present during their own training. This failure is not simply explained by absence of the concept from the subject or oracle representations: the target remains decodable inside the oracle, while LogitLens and layer-ablation analyses indicate that the failure arises in the AO readout pathway. Our results show that behavioral leakage, representation-level decodability, and AO-verbalizability can come apart, raising a reliability concern for learned interpretability interfaces.

</details>


### [212] [LA-RL: Label-Aware Self-Reflection for Reinforcement Learning in Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23420)
*Xiao You,Tianwei Yan,Zixu Shan,Longyu Du,Shan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LA-RL框架，通过任务感知的诊断标签引导信息抽取自我纠正，在实体识别、关系抽取和事件抽取上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反思的纠正方法在结构化抽取任务中存在对齐问题，无法准确识别失败类型（如缺失跨度、标签错误、边界不匹配等），需要更细粒度的诊断引导。

Method: 使用单一骨干网络依次完成预测、诊断错误标签、条件修订；从标注模型生成的诊断数据冷启动，再通过两阶段GRPO强化学习优化最终质量、格式有效性和首轮正确性。

Result: 在NER、关系抽取和事件抽取任务上一致超越SFT基线，SciER关系抽取平均F1提升6.83，OoD关系抽取提升约20 F1，DuEE1.0上触发词F1提升14.80、论元F1提升17.50。

Conclusion: 反思结构应任务敏感：关系抽取需要强约束，NER在领域迁移下需要更宽松的纠正策略。

Abstract: Large language models show strong promise for information extraction (IE), but existing reflection-based correction methods are often misaligned with structured extraction outputs. Free-form self-reflection can flag an error, yet it rarely identifies whether the failure is a missing span, wrong label, boundary mismatch, invalid relation type, or reversed argument order. We introduce LA-RL (Label-Aware Reflective Reinforcement Learning), an outcome-supervised framework that guides IE self-correction with task-grounded diagnostic labels. A single backbone first predicts an extraction, diagnoses task-specific error labels, and then revises its output conditioned on the diagnosis. Training starts from diagnostic data labeled by an annotation model for cold-start supervised fine-tuning and proceeds through two GRPO stages that reward final extraction quality, format validity, and first-pass correctness, without a process reward model. Experiments on named entity recognition, relation extraction, and event extraction show consistent same-backbone gains over SFT, including 6.83 average F1 on SciER relation extraction, about 20 F1 on out-of-distribution relation extraction, and 14.80 trigger F1 plus 17.50 argument F1 on DuEE1.0. Ablations show that reflection structure is task-sensitive: stronger constraints benefit relation extraction, whereas named entity recognition needs less restrictive correction under domain shift.

</details>


### [213] [Reasoning or Memorization: Can LLMs Understand and Generate Chinese Xiehouyu Riddles?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23440)
*Hai Hu,Siyuan Song,Chongtian Shao,Kejia Zhang,Tianjian Zhu,Xiaojing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过中文歇后语游戏测试LLM推理能力，发现前沿中文模型存在严重数据记忆问题（Δacc 23.6%），而英文模型记忆较少（Δacc 5.1%）；Gemini 3.1 Pro在新歇后语理解上超越人类24%，但在创造性生成上远不及人类专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理能力评估常受数据污染影响，需要设计能排除记忆效应的测试方法来真正检验模型的推理与创造能力。

Method: 使用语言学家创作的全新歇后语（避免数据污染），通过多选题（用Δacc衡量记忆程度）、自由解释生成和新歇后语创作三方面评估LLM，并将表现与人类进行对比。

Result: 中文前沿模型Δacc达23.6%（记忆严重），英文模型仅5.1%；Gemini 3.1 Pro在新歇后语理解上准确率92.6%，比人类高24%；LLM创作的歇后语评分远低于人类创作。

Conclusion: LLM的推理能力声称需重新审视数据污染问题，在语言相关创造性任务上，LLM仍落后于人类专家。

Abstract: In this paper, we push the boundary of LLM reasoning by testing them in a Chinese language game, xiehouyu, with novel xiehouyu created by linguists that had not existed before to avoid data contamination. We use multiple-choice questions (MCQ), free-form explanation generation, and new xiehouyu creation to evaluate LLMs' ability to understand and create xiehouyu. In MCQ, we use the delta of accuracy ($Δ_{acc}$) between existing but low-frequency xiehouyu and novel ones as an index for memorization. $Δ_{acc}$ for native speakers is very low, suggesting similar processing mechanisms. However, we found that frontier Chinese models have on average a $Δ_{acc}$ of 23.6\%, while English-centric models tested have a mean $Δ_{acc}$ of 5.1\%, suggesting that frontier Chinese models are likely trained with much larger Chinese data, thus memorizing more low-frequency xiehouyu. For novel xiehouyu, Gemini 3.1 Pro demonstrated remarkable ability with acc 92.6, which is 24\% higher than human accuracy. In xiehouyu creation, those created by LLMs receive much worse ratings than those by humans. These results suggest that claims about the reasoning abilities of LLMs may need careful re-examination considering the data contamination issue, and that LLMs' creativity in language-related tasks may still be behind human experts, at least in Chinese xiehouyu.

</details>


### [214] [Do LLM Debates Repeat Arguments Differently Across Languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23442)
*Huiqian Lai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过"先验论点相似度"这一诊断指标，研究多语言LLM辩论中论点发展模式，发现中文辩论相比英文存在持续且显著更高的论点重复倾向，且该差距在多种控制条件下依然稳定存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM辩论评估通常只关注最终答案，但辩论过程文本中包含丰富的论证发展信息。作者希望系统性地衡量辩论过程中论点是否在"发展新内容"还是"重复旧观点"，并探究不同语言下这一模式是否存在差异。

Method: 提出"先验论点相似度"指标，将辩论中的论点单元与之前轮次的论点进行相似度比较。在71个议题、6种语言、4种模型智能体的8轮辩论中，使用三种多语言嵌入模型进行跨语言对比分析，并进行了回归调整、指标变体、提取长度控制、交叉编码器尾部重评分等多种稳健性检验。

Result: 中文是唯一一种在三种多语言嵌入模型下与英文相比始终呈现正向相似度差距的语言。该差距在模型智能体、轮次位置、指标变体、提取长度控制等条件下均持续存在。手动校准显示项目级对齐较弱，但高相似度尾部明显包含实质性重复。多样性提示虽能降低各语言的先验论点相似度，但并未显著缩小中英差距。

Conclusion: 多语言辩论评估应衡量论点的论证发展过程（而不仅仅是最终答案），并在报告评估结果时同时报告平均值和语言间差距，以更好地捕捉论证质量的语言差异。

Abstract: LLM debate is usually evaluated by final answers, but transcripts also reveal whether later turns develop new argumentative content or return to earlier claims in new wording. We study this process with \textit{prior-argument similarity}, an aggregate diagnostic comparing extracted argument units with earlier units in the same debate. In controlled eight-turn debates over 71 motions, six languages, and four model agents, Chinese is the only tested language with a consistently positive gap relative to English across three multilingual embedding models. The gap persists across agents, turn positions, regression adjustment, metric variants, extraction-length controls, a second-extractor subset, and cross-encoder tail rescoring. Manual calibration shows weak item-level alignment but a high-similarity tail enriched for substantive repetition. A diversity-aware prompt lowers prior-argument similarity across languages, yet does not significantly narrow the Chinese--English gap. These findings suggest that multilingual debate evaluation should measure argumentative development over time and report mitigation effects in both average and gap terms.

</details>


### [215] [Do Small Models Use the Law You Give Them? Context-Injected Fine-Tuning for Legal QA in Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23446)
*Moniruzzaman Mahadi,Abrar Mohammed Tanzim Alam,Sayma Siddika Monalisa,Mir Mohammad Asif Abdullah,Swakkhar Shatabda,Md Adnan Arefeen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过对孟加拉法律QA数据集微调Qwen3.5（0.8B-4B），发现微调能提升小模型对检索法律的利用能力，减少语言漂移，但4B模型在英文条件下出现倒退，表明检索质量并非唯一瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型即使接收到相关法律条文仍可能回答错误，作者希望探究微调是否能改善模型对检索到的法律信息的利用能力。

Method: 构建2,165条双语QA数据集（6部孟加拉法案+3个附表），对Qwen3.5（0.8B、2B、4B）进行微调，使用2022和2023年孟加拉律师资格考试题目（孟加拉语+机器翻译英语）评估，采用BM25和FAISS检索，三次运行严格一致性评分。

Result: 0.8B模型在2022年英语FAISS得分从2/100提升至34/100；0.8B和2B模型提升显著，但4B模型无净增益（孟加拉语提升但英文条件退步）；微调将语言漂移（从孟加拉语混入英语）从44.0-53.2%降至0.2-0.7%。

Conclusion: 检索质量不是唯一瓶颈，小型双语法律模型在利用已提供法律和按要求语言回答方面存在差异。

Abstract: A small language model can receive the governing statutory provision and still answer incorrectly. We test whether fine-tuning on examples containing relevant law improves later use of retrieved law. We curate 2{,}165 bilingual QA records from six Bangladeshi acts and three schedules, then fine-tune Qwen3.5 at 0.8B, 2B, and 4B. Evaluation uses the 2022 and 2023 Bangladesh Bar Council exams in Bangla and machine-translated English, with no retrieval, BM25, or FAISS, scored by strict consistency over three seeded runs. At 0.8B, fine-tuning raises the 2022 English FAISS score from 2 to 34 of 100. Gains at 0.8B and 2B survive paired testing, but the 4B model has no detectable net gain: Bangla improves while several English conditions regress. Fine-tuning also reduces answers that drift from Bangla into mostly English from 44.0--53.2\% to 0.2--0.7\%, with adjusted $p<.001$ at every scale. Retrieval quality is therefore not the only bottleneck. Small bilingual legal models also differ in how they use supplied law and whether they answer in the requested language. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/momahadi/bangladesh-legal-qa-dataset.

</details>


### [216] [Two Regimes of Chain-of-Thought Unfaithfulness: Behavioral Detection Fails Where Models Are Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23458)
*Suramya R. Angdembay,Dikshant Aryal,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统性地验证了答案正确性在CoT faithfulness检测中的核心作用：错误答案中集中了69%的不忠实推理，且现有检测信号在此场景下全部失效；正确答案场景下信号仅中等有效；线性探针与跨场景迁移实验进一步揭示了检测盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于评估链式思维解释的忠实性（即推理过程是否真实导致最终答案），并发现现有黑盒检测方法的局限性，尤其是答案正确性如何影响检测效果。

Method: 使用FaithCoT-Bench人工标注数据，对比多种黑盒检测信号；通过答案正确性分层分析；使用线性探针解码内部表示；跨模型和跨场景迁移实验；验证并修正了基准的文档-数据标签不匹配问题。

Result: 答案错误与否本身（AUROC 0.696）优于所有专用检测信号；69%不忠实发生在错误答案上；正确答案上检测信号中等有效（0.63-0.67），错误答案上全部失效；step-removal指标与人标注反相关；线性探针在不同场景和模型间无共享正向方向；检测信号无法跨场景迁移。

Conclusion: 现有CoT忠实性检测方法在错误答案场景下完全失效，核心问题由答案正确性结构主导；需要针对不同正确性场景开发专门的忠实性检测方法。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) explanations support oversight only if they are faithful: the stated reasoning must actually produce the answer. Auditing black-box (behavioral) detection of unfaithful CoT against FaithCoT-Bench's human annotations, we find answer correctness structures the problem at every level. Answer incorrectness alone (an oracle diagnostic, not a deployable detector) outperforms every purpose-built signal (AUROC 0.696), because 69% of annotated unfaithfulness occurs on incorrect answers. Stratifying by correctness splits detection into two regimes: on correct answers, behavioral signals moderately separate faithful from post-hoc reasoning (0.63-0.67); on incorrect answers, where most unfaithfulness lives, no tested signal is detectably above chance (replicated on all four models for benchmark-wide signals). The standard step-removal metric anti-correlates with human labels; this inversion reproduces on the benchmark's released scores and on hint-dependent counterfactually labeled traces.
